      "content": "**Key instructions**:\n(1) Make sure to end code blocks with ``` followed by a newline(\n).\n(2) Remember you can use the variables in your code in subsequent code blocks.\n(3) Remember that the email addresses, access tokens and variables (e.g. spotify_password) in the example above are not valid anymore.\n(4) You can use the \"supervisor\" app to get information about my accounts and use the \"phone\" app to get information about friends and family.\n(5) Always look at API specifications (using apis.api_docs.show_api_doc) before calling an API.\n(6) Write small chunks of code and only one chunk of code in every step. Make sure everything is working correctly before making any irreversible change.\n(7) Many APIs return items in \"pages\". Make sure to run through all the pages by looping over `page_index`.\n(8) Once you have completed the task, make sure to call apis.supervisor.complete_task().   "raw_model_response": "To determine the total number of likes on all Venmo transactions I sent this month, I need to follow these steps:\n\n1. **Get Venmo credentials**: Use the supervisor app to retrieve the Venmo password.\n2. **Login to Venmo**: Use the retrieved password and my email to log in.\n3. **Access transaction data**: Use the Venmo API to fetch all transactions I sent this month.\n4. **Count likes**: For each transaction, count the number of likes and sum them up.\n\nLet me start by exploring the available Venmo APIs to understand what I can do.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

  "raw_model_response": "To determine the total number of likes on all Venmo transactions I sent this month, I need to follow these steps:\n\n1. **Get Venmo credentials**: Use the supervisor app to retrieve the Venmo password.\n2. **Login to Venmo**: Use the retrieved password and my email to log in.\n3. **Access transaction data**: Use the Venmo API to fetch all transactions I sent this month.\n4. **Count likes**: For each transaction, count the number of likes and sum them up.\n\nLet me start by exploring the available Venmo APIs to understand what I can do.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

I'll also use pagination to ensure I get all transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\npage_limit = 100  # Set a reasonable page limit to minimize API calls\n\nwhile True:\n    response = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'],\n        direction=\"sent\",\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index,\n        page_limit=page_limit\n    )\n    \n    if not response.get(\"transactions\"):\n        break\n    \n    transactions.extend(response[\"transactions\"])\n    page_index += 1\n\nprint(f\"Total transactions sent this month: {len(transactions)}\")\n```",
